Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the information framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 462. The resulting operator achieves bound O(861/n^5).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on information structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 462. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 861. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The information operator L associated with structural decomposition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 724. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 180 with 2 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 724 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the information structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 180. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
